आदेश 
का . आ . 588. - यतः निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया 
है कि लोक सभा के लिए निर्वाचन के लिए 3 झनझन संसदीय 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से चुनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार श्री पुलाराम सुपुत्र 
श्री खेताराम , डाकघर चिराव , जिला झनझन, राजस्थान लोक प्रति 
निधित्व अधिनियम , 1951 तथा सदधीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा 
अपेक्षित समय के अन्दर तथा रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा 
दाखिल करने में असफल रहे है , 

और, यतः, उक्त उम्मीदवार ने, उसे सम्पक सूचना दिये जाने 
पर भी , अपनी इस असफलता के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टी 
करण नहीं दिया है , और निर्वाचन आयोग का यह भी समाधान 
हो गया है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त 
कारण या न्यायोचिस्य नहीं है : 
____ अतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10-क के अनुसरण 
म निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा उक्त श्री सुला राम को संसद के 
किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान सभा अथवा 
विधान परिषद् के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश 
की तारीख तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए निरीहत घोषित करता

S .O . 588. — Whereas the Election Commission is satis 
fied that Shri Dula Ram S / o Shri Kheta Ram , Post Chirawa , 
District Jhunjhunu , Rajasthan , a contesting candidate for 
election to the House of the People from 3 - Jhunjhunu Par 
liamentary Constituency, has failed to lodge an account of 
his election expenses within the time and in the manner 
as required by the Representation of the People Act , 1951 , 
and the Rules made thereunder ;

951 . 


2 . And whereas, the said candidate even after the notices 
has not given any reason or explanation for the failure and 
the Election Commission is satisfied that he has no good 
reason or justification for such failuro ; 


और, यतः, उक्त उम्मीदवार ने, उसे सम्यक सूचना दिये जाने पर 
भी अपनी इस असफलता के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण 
नहीं दिया है , और निर्वाचन आयोग का यह भी समाधान हो गया 
है कि उस के पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या 
न्यायोचित्य नहीं है । 


3. Now , therefore , in pursuance of section 10 A of the 
said Act, the Election Commission hereby declares the said 
Shri Dula Ram to be disqualified for being chosen as 
and for being a member of either House of Parliament or of 
the Legislative Assembly or Legislative Council of a State 
for a period of three years from the datę of this order.

साधारण निर्वाचन के लिए 31 -गोधो निर्वाचन-क्षेश से पनाग लाइन 
पाले उम्मीदवार श्री पटेल मुलजीभाई नानालाल , मंडप कांट्रेक्टर , 
कापड़ बाजार, भावनगर ( गुजरास ) लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम , 
1951 तथा तबधीन बनाए गए नियमों यारा अपेक्षित रीति से अपने 
निर्वाचन व्यों का लेखा पाखिल करम में असफल रहे है 


ऑर, यसः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने , उसे सम्यक सूचना विर्थ 
जाने पर भी , अपनी इस असफलसा के लिए कोई कारण अथवा 
स्पष्टीकरण नहीं पिथा है , और, निर्वाचन आयोग का यह भी समाधान 
हो गया है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण 
या न्यायोचित्य नहीं है , .

संसद के किसी भी सपन के या किसी राज्य की विधान-सभा अथवा 
विधान परिषद के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश 
की सारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए निराईत घोषित 
करता है ।

श्री इन्द्र बहादुर ठाकरी, बेलगाची टी . एस्टेट, डा . बेल गाची, दार्जीिलंग , 
प . बंगाल लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम , 1951 तथा तद्धीन 
बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का कोई

हो गया है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त 
कारण था न्यायोचित्य नहीं है ।

के किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान सभा अथवा विधान 
परिषद् के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश की तारीख

घोषित करता है ।

* नकदी, प्रावधिक जमा और अल्पकालीन प्रतिभूतियां शामिल हैं । 
* * राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन कियायें ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय औद्योगिक ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन क्रियायें ) निधि में से किये गये निवेश शामिल नहीं हैं । 
@, राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन क्रियायें ) निधि से प्रदस ऋण और अग्रिम शामिल नहीं हैं , परन्तु राज्य सरकारों को दिये गये अस्थायी प्रोवरड्राफ्ट शामिल हैं । 
रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ इंडिया अधिनियम की धारा 17 ( 4 ) ( ग ) के अधीन अनुसूचित वाणिज्य बैंकों को मीयादी बिलों पर अग्रिम दिये गये 40, 00),()0 0 रपये शामिल हैं । 

राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण (दीर्घकालीन क्रियायें ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण (स्थिरीकरण ) निधि से प्रदत्त ऋण और अग्रिम शामिल नहीं हैं ।

* नकवी, प्रावधिक जमा और अल्पकालीन प्रतिभूतियां शामिल हैं । 
* * राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण (दीर्घकालीन क्रियाएं ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय प्रोद्योगिक ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन श्रियाएं ) निधि में से किये गये निवेश शामिल नहीं है । 

@ राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन क्रियाएं ) निधि से प्रदत्त ऋण और अग्रिम शामिल नहीं हैं , परन्तु राज्य सरकारों को दिये गये अस्थायी प्रोवरापट शामिल हैं । 
रिजर्व बौक ऑफ इंडिया अधिनियम की धारा 17 ( 4 ) ( ग ) के अधीन वाणिज्य बैंकों को मियादी बिलों पर अग्रिम दिये गये 60,()(), 000 रूपये शामिल है 
राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋग (बोर्वकालीन क्रियाएं ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण (स्थिरीकरण ) निधि से प्रवत्त ऋण और अग्रिम शामिल नहीं हैं ।

केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क तथा सीमा - शुल्क उप - समहता कार्यालय 

जयपुर, 12 फरवरी, 1973 

( केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क ) 
का० प्रा० 6 21: —- केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 15 और 16 के अन्तर्गत मुझ प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रगोग करते हुये और इम 
कार्यालय द्वारा जारी को गई अधिसूचना सो० संख्या V ( 4 ) 30/ 1/ इन्ट / G8/ 111/ 23783दिनांक 6 दिगम्बर 1966 8, और अधिसूचना सी . संध्या V ( + ) 30/ 1, इन्ट 
68/ 111/ 50- 86दिनांक 2 जनवरी , 1969 में प्रांशिक संशोधन करते हुये मैं एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करता हूं कि ऐसे अनिर्मित तम्बाकू के सम्बन्ध में , जिसकी 
इस अधिसूचना के साथ संलग्न अनुसूची के कालम 7 में निर्दिष्ट सीमा से अनधिक भूमि में खेती की जाती है , या जो उक्त अनुसूची के कालम 8 में उल्लि 
खित सीमा से अनधिक मात्रा में सुखाया जाता है , केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क संभाग जोधपुर के कालम 5 में उल्लिखित सम्बद्ध राजस्व महसीला के सम्पूर्ण राजस्व 
क्षेत्र में उक्त नियमों के अन्तर्गत कोई घोषणा प्रावश्यक नहीं होगी । 

अनुसूची 
केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क नियम, 1944 के नियम 15- 16 के अधीन जारी की गई अधिसूचना की अनुमुची

का० प्रा० 6 22. - केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 1 5 और 16 के अन्तर्गत मुझे प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और इस कार्यालय द्वारा जारी की 
गई अधिसूचना स० संख्या V ( 4) 30/1/इन्ट/ 68/III/ 23752-23783 दिनांक 6 दिसम्बर, 1968, और अधिसूचना सी० संख्या V ( 4 ) 30/ 1/ इन्ट / 68 / ITI/ 50-86 
दिनांक 2 जनवरी 1969 में आंशिक संशोधन करते हुए मैं एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करता हूं कि ऐसे प्रनिर्मित तम्बाकू के संबंध में , जिसकी इस अधिसूचना के साथ संलग्न 
अनुसूची के कालम 7 में निर्विष्ट सीमा से अधिक भूमि में खेती की जाती है, या जो उक्त अनुसूची के कालम 8 में उल्लिखित सीमा से अनधिक मात्रा में सुखाया जाता है, केन्द्रीय 
उत्पादन शुल्क संभाग जयपुर के कालम 5 में उलिखित संबंध राजस्व तहसीलों के सम्पूर्ण राजस्व क्षेत्र में उक्त नियमों के अन्तर्गत कोई षोषणा प्रावश्यक नहीं होगी । 


केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 15 - 16 के अधीन आरी की गई अधिसूचना 


सेक्टर का नाम

छूट दी गई है ।

मा . प्रा . 623. - - केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क नियम 1944 के नियम 15 और 16 के अन्तर्गत मुझे प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और इस कार्यालय द्वारा जारी 
की गई अधिसूचना संख्या सी0 V( 4 ) 30/ 1/ इन्ट / 68/ 3/ III / 23752 - 23783, दिनांक 6 विसम्बर, 1968, मौर प्रधिसूचना सी० संख्या 5 ( 4 ) 30/ 1/ इन्ट / 68/ 3/III 
50 - 80 दिनांक 2 जनवरी , 1989 में मांशिक संशोधन करते हुए मैं एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करता हूं कि ऐसे प्रनिमित्त लम्बाकू के संबंध में , जिसकी इस 
अधिसूचना के साथ संलग्न अनुसूची के कालम 7 में निर्दिष्ट सीमा से अनधिक भूमि में खेती की जाती है, या जो उफ्त अनुसूची के फालम 8 में उल्लिखित 
सीमा से मनधिक मात्रा में सुखाया जाता है, केन्द्रीय उत्पादन शुल्क संभाग कोटा के कालम 5 में उल्लिखित सम्बद राजस्व तहसीलों के सम्पूर्ण राजस्व क्षेत्र 
में उस नियमों के अन्तर्गत कोई घोषणा प्रावश्यक नहीं होगी । 


केन्द्रीय उस्पादन शुल्क नियम , 1944 के नियम 15- 16 के अधीन जारी की गई अधिसूचना की अनुसूची

उत्पादन शुल्क नियम 16 के 
नियम 1944 के अन्तर्गत बिना 
अन्तर्गत बिना पूर्वोक्त घोषणा 
घोषणा किए काश्त - झिये कालम 6

में तम्बाकू की खेती 
कर सकता है

मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं० का० नि . पा . . 631, तारीख 28 फरवरी, 1937 को प्रधिक्रान्त करते हुए , एतद्वारा निम्नलिखित निदेश देते हैं कि : 
( 1 ) इस प्रादेश की अनुसूची के भाग । के स्तम्भ ( 1 ) में विनिविष्ट साधारण केन्द्रीय सेवा, पर्ग II में पदों के बारे में , स्तम्भ ( 2 ) में निविष्ट 

प्राधिकारी , नियुक्ति प्राधिकारी होगा, और स्तम्भ ( 3 ) में विमिविष्ट प्राधिकारी , स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट शास्तियों के बारे में अनुशासन प्राधिकारीहोगा ; 
( 2) उपत अनुसूची के भाग II मोर भाग III के स्तम्भ ( 1 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट साधारण केन्द्रीय सेवा , मर्ग III , और साधारण केन्द्रीम सेषा , वर्ग IVD 
- पदों के बारे में स्तम्भ ( 2 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट प्राधिकारी , क्रमशः नियुक्ति प्रधकारी होंगे . पौर स्तम्भ : ( 3 ) मोर ( 5 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट प्राधिकारी, 
स्तम्भ ( 4 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट शक्तियों के बारे में क्रमशः प्रमुशासन प्राधिकारी और अपील प्राधिकारी होंगे ।

MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 

ORDER 


New Delhi, the 20th February , 1973 


S . O . 624 . - -In exercise of the powors conferred by sub -rulo (2) of rule, 9 clause (b ) of sub -rule (2 ) of rulo 12 and sub-lule (1) 
of rulo 24 of the Contral Civil Servicos (Classification Control and Appeal) Rulos, 1965, and in supersession of the late Ministry of Cim 
merce and Consumer Industries Notification No . S. R. O . 631, dated the 28th February, 1957, the President hereby directs that 

(1) in respect of the posts in the General Contral Service, Class II , specificd in column ( 1) of Part I of the Schedule to this Order . 
the authority spocifled in column (2) shall be the Appointing Authority and the authority specified in column (3 ) shall be the Disciplinary 
Authority in regard to the penalties specified in column ( 4) ; 

(2) in respect of the posts in the Ganoral Contral Service , Class III and the Goneral Central Service , Class IV , specified in column 
(1) of Part II and Part III of tho said Schedule respectively , the authority specified in column (2) shall be the Appointing Authority , and 
the authoritlos spocified in columns (3) and (3) shall be the Disciplinary Authority and Appellate Authority respectively in regard to the 
penaltios specifiedin column( 4).

का . आ . 828. - नियत ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) अधि 
नियम, 1983 (1963 का 22 ) की धारा 17 धारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार, नारियल-जटा से मन सूत का 
निर्यात (निरीक्षण) नियम , 1988 में संशोधन करने के लिए 
एसद्वारा निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है , अर्थात : 


1. ( 1) इन नियमों का नाम नारियल - जटा से मन सूत का 
निर्यात (निरीक्षण ) संशोधन नियम , 1973 है । 


( 2 ) ये राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख को प्रवृस्त होंगे । 


का . आ . 828 . -निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण) अधि 
नियम , 1963 (1983 का 22 ) की धारा 17 वारा प्रपत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार, नारियल - जटा के बने उत्पादों का 
निर्यात (निरीक्षण) नियम , 1985 में संशोधित करने के लिए 
एतद्द्वारा निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है , अर्थात : 

1. ( 1) इन नियमों का नाम नारियल - जटा से बने उत्पादों का 
निर्याप्त (निरीक्षण ) संशाधन नियम , 1973 है । 

( 2) ये राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख को प्रवृत्त होंगे । 
. .. 2. नारियल-जटा से बने उत्पादों का निर्यात (निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1965 में , नियम 4 के उप -नियम ( 4) के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित 
नियम रखा जाएगा, अर्थात् : 
"( 4) अभिकरण अपना यह समाधान हो जाने के पश्चात् 

कि नारियल -जटा से बने उत्पादों का परषण मान्यता 
प्राप्त विनिर्देशों के अनुरूप है , नियम 4 के उप-नियम 
( 1) के अधीन प्रशापना और परेषण की विशिष्टियों के 
प्राप्त होने के तीन दिन के अन्दर परेषण को मान्यता प्राप्त 
विनिशों के अनुरूप तथा निर्यात- योग्य घोषित करते 

हए निर्यातकर्ता को एक प्रमाणपत्र वेगा । 
परन्त, जहां अभिकरण का इस प्रकार समाधान नहीं हो पाता 

है , वहां वह उक्त तीन दिन की अवधि के अन्दर ऐसा 
प्रमाण-पत्र देने से इन्कार कर देगा और इंकार करने के 
कारण देते हुए उसकी संसूचना निर्यातकर्ता को लंगा । "

New Delhi , the 3rd March, 1973 
S. O . 625. - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 
17 of the Export ( Quality Control and Inspection) Act, 1963 
( 22 of 1963 ), the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules to amend the Export of Coir Products 
(Inspection) Rules, 1965, namely : 

1. (1) Thesc rules may be Called thoExport of Coir Pro 
ducts (Inspection ) Amendment Rulos , 1973. 

( li) They shall come into force on the date of their pub 
lication in the official gazette . 

2 . In tho Export of Coir Products ( Inspection ) Rules, 1965, 
for sub - rule ( 4 ) of rule 4 , the following rule shall be sub . 
stituted namoly : 

" ( 4 ) After satisfying itself that the consignment of Coir 
Products conforms to the recognised specifications the agency 


2. नारियल -जटा से बने सूत का निर्यात (निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1988 में , नियम 7 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित नियम रखा जाएगा , 
अर्थात् : 


" 7. निरीक्षण का प्रमाण-पत्र : - अभिकरण अपना यह समाधान 

हो जाने के पश्चात् कि नारियल जटा से बने सूत का 
परेषण मान्यताप्राप्त विनिर्देशों के अनुरूप है और इस 
निमित्त दिए गए अनपेशों के अनुसार पैक किया गया 
है तथा उस पर लेबल लगाया गया है , नियम 4 के उप 
नियम (1 ) के अधीन प्रज्ञापना और परेषण की 
विशिष्टियों के प्राप्त होने के तीन दिन के अन्दर परेषण 
को मान्यताप्राप्त विनिर्देशों के अनुरूप तथा निर्यात -योग्य 
घोषित करते हएनिर्यातकर्ता को एक प्रमाण -पत्र देगा । 


परन्त जहां अभिकरण का इस प्रकार समाधान नहीं हो पाता 

है , वहां वह उक्त तीन दिन की अवधि के अन्दर ऐसा 
प्रमाण-पत्र देने से इन्कार कर देगा और इंकार करने के 
कारण देते हुए उसकी संसुचना निर्यातकर्ता को देगा । "

MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 

NOTIFICATION 

New Delhi, the 3rd March , 1973 
S .O . 626 . - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 
17 of the Export (Quality Control and Inspection ) Act, 1963 
( 22 of 1963 ), the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules to amend the Export of Coir Yarn 
(Inspection) Rules, 1966 , namely : 

1. (i) These rules may be called the Export of Coir Yarn 
(Inspection ) Amendment Rules, 1973 . 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date of their Pub 
lication in the official gazette . 

2. In the Export of Coir Yarn (Inspection ) Rules , 1966 , 
for rule 7 , the following rule shall be substituted , namely : 

" 7 . Certificate of Inspection - After satisfying itself that the 
consignment of Coir Yarn conforms to the recognised speci 
fications and has been packed and labelled in accordance with 
the instructions issued in this behalf, the agency shall within 
three days of the receipt of the intimation and the parti 
culars of the consignment under sub-rule ( 1 ) of rule 4. issuc 
a certificato to the exporter declaring that tho consignment 
conforms to the recogniscd specification and is export 
worthy . 

Provided that where the agency is not so satisfied, it shall 
within the said period of three days refuse to issue such 
certificate and communicate such refusal to the exporter 
alongwith the reasons therefore ." 

[ No. 6( 5)/ 71- EI & EP] 
M . K . B . BHATNAGAR, Dy . Director, 

(Export Promotion ). 


(Office of the Chief Controller of Imports and Exports) 

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 17th February , 1973 
S .O . 627. — M /s . Piem Hotels Pvt. Ltd ., Neel Kanth , 
98, Marine Drives, Bombay - 2 were granted an import licence 

No. P / A / 1357417 dated 13 - 10- 71 for Rs. 10, 000 ( Rupees 
Ten Thousand only ). They have applied for thọ issue of 
duplicate Exchange Control Purposes copy of the said licenco 
on the ground that the original Exchange Control copy has 
been lost. It is further stated that the original Exchange 
Control Copy was registered with the Customs authorities 
at Bombay and utilised partly . It was utilised for 
Rs. 9 , 672.68 and the balance available on it was Rs. 327 . 32 . 

2 . In support of this contention , the applicant has filed an 
affidavit on a stamped paper . I am accordingly satisfied that 
the original Exchange Control Purposes copy of the said 
licence has been lost. Therefore in exercise of the powers 
conferred under Sub-clause 9 ( cc ) of the Imports ( Control ) 
Order 1955 dated 7 - 12- 1955 as amended, the said original 
Exchange Control Purposes copy of Licence No . P / A / 
1357417 dated 13 - 10 - 71 issued to M / s. Piem Hotels Pvt . 
Ltd ., Bombay is hereby cancelled . 

3 . A duplicate Exchange Control Purposes Copy of the 
said licence is being issued separately to the licensce . 

___ [ No . 469- H/ AM- 72 /ILS / 3943] 
M. G. GOMBAR, Dy . Chief Controller . 


(मुख्य निमंत्रक, आषात-निर्यात का कार्यालण ) 


आदेश 
नई दिल्ली , 17 फरवरी, 1973 
का . आ . 627 . - सर्वश्री पाइम होटल्स प्रा . लि ., नील कंठ, 98, 
मरिन ड्राइज, बम्बई -2 को 10, 000 रु . ( दस हजार रु. मात्र ) के 
लिए एक आयात लाइसेंस सं . पी / ए/ 1357417 दिनांक 13-10- 71 
स्वीकृत किया गया था । उन्होंने उपर्यक्त लाइसेंस की अनलिपि 
मना विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रति के लिए इस आधार पर आवेदन किया 
है कि मूल मझा विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रति खो गई है । आगे यह 
बताया गया है कि मूल मना यिनिमय नियंत्रण प्रति सम्बई 
सीमाशुल्क प्राधिकारियों के पास पंजीकृत करवाई गई थी और 
उसका अंशतः उपयोग किया गया था । इसका 9, 672.68 रु. के 
लिए उपयोग किया गया था और शेष 327.32 रु. बाकी था । 

2. इस तर्क के समर्थन में आवेदक ने स्टाम्प कागज पर एक 
शपथ दाखिल किया है । मैं , सदानुसार संतुष्ट हूं कि उक्त 
लाइसेंस की मूल मद्रा विनिमय नियंत्रण प्रति खो गई है । 
इसलिए, यथा संशोधित आयात (नियंत्रण ) आदेश , 1955 
दिनांक 7-12-1955 की उप- धारा ( सी सी ) के अंतर्गत प्रदत्त

पूर्ण मूल्य क्रमशः 14, 8 1 3 रुपये और 1 4, 8 1 2 रुपये के लिए इस आधार पर

निकासी प्रतियां खो गई हैं । यह भी उल्लेख किया गया है कि मूल लाइसेंस 
म सो किसी सीमाशुल्क कार्यालय में पंजीकृत कराए थे और न उन के पूरे 
मुल्यों --- क्रमश : 14, 81 3 रुपये मोर 14, 8 1 2 रुपये की सीमा तक उन्होंने उनका 
उपयोग किया था ।

का० प्रा० 637 - - यतः इस संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट और पैट्रो 
लियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का प्रजन ) अधिनियम , 
1962 की धारा 6 की उप-धारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित भारत सरकार 
की अधिसूचना द्वारा गुजरात राज्य के कलोल तेल क्षेत्र में व्यधन स्थल

और यत : सेल और प्राकृतिक गैस भायोग ने 25- 9- 71 को 
उक्त प्रधिनियम की धारा 7 की उप-धारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( i ) में निदिष्ट 
संक्रिया को पर्यवसित कर दिया है । 


अब यतः पट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का 
अर्जन ) नियमावली , 1963 के नियम 4 के अधीन सभम प्राधिकारी उक्त . 
तारीख को ऊपर निर्दिष्ट संक्रिया के पर्यवसान के रूप में एतदद्वारा अधि 
सूचित करता है । 


और यत : तेल और प्राकृतिक गैम प्रायोग ने 29- 3-1971 को 
उक्त मधिनियम की धारा 7 की उप-धारा ( 1 ) के खान ( 1 ) में निर्दिष्ट 
संत्रिया को पर्यवसित कर दिया है ।

New Delhi, the 15th February 1973. 
S . O . 635. - Whereas it appears to the Central Gover 
nment that it is necessary in the public interest thatfor the trans 
port of Petroleum Products between the Haldia Port (District 
Midnapore ) and Rajbandh Delivery Point (District- Burdwan ) 
of Indian Oil Corporation Limited ( Pipelines ) in West Bengal 
States, pipelines should be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation 
Limited (Pipelines) and that for the purpose of laying such pipo 
lines it is necessary to acquire the right of user in the land descri 
bed in the Schedule annexed hereto : 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
section (1 ) of Section 3 of the Petrolcum Pipclines ( Acquisition 
of Right User in land ) Act , 1962 (50 of 1962 ) the Central Go 
vernment hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of 
user therein . 
___ Any personinterested in the said land may within 21 (Twenty 
one ) days from the date of this Notification object to the laying 
of pipelines under the land , to Competent Authority at 14 , Lee 
Road, Calcutta - 20 , in the office of the Indian Oil Corporation 
Linited ( Pipelines ). Every person making such an objection 
shili also stato specifically whether he wishes to be heard in 
person or by legal practitioner ,

का० प्रा० 638. - - अतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह 
प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि हल्दिया 
पोर्ट (जिला मिदनापुर ) और पश्चिम बंगाल राज्य में भारतीय तेल 
निगम ( पाइपलाइन्स ) के राजगंध वितरण केन्द्र ( जिला बरदवान ) के 
बीच पैट्रोलियम उत्पादों के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन भारतीय तेल 
निगम ( पाइपलाइन्स ) द्वारा मिलाई जानी चाहिए और कि ऐसी लाइनों 
को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एतद्पाबद्ध प्रमुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करना आवश्यक है । 

प्रतः प्रब , पैट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का 
अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस 
में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राशय एतदद्वारा घोषित 
किया है । 

उक्त भूमि में हितबर कोई व्यक्ति उस भूमि के नीचे पाइपलाइन 
बिछाने के लिये प्राक्षेप भारतीय तेल निगम लि . ( पाइपलाइन्स ) के 
कार्यालय में सक्षम प्राधिकारी , 14, ली रोड कलकत्ता - 20 को इस अधि 
सूचमा की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । ऐसा माक्षेप 
फरने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्ट; यह भी कथन करेगा कि क्या वह 
यह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिशः हो या किसी विधि व्यवसायी 
की मार्फत ।

S . O . 639 . -- WHEREAS by a notification of the Government 
of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Chemicals (Depart 
ment of Petroleum) S . O . No . 3179 dated 14- 9 - 1972 , under 
sub - section ( 1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum Pipelincs 
( Acquisition of Right of User in land ) Act , 1962 ( 50 of 
1962), the Central Government declared its intention to 
acquire the Right of User in thc lands specified in the 
schedule appended to that notification for the purpose of lay 
ing pipelines ; 

AND WHEREAS the Competent Authority has under sub 
section ( 1) of section 6 of the said Act, submitted report to 
the Government ; 

AND FURTHER WHEREAS the Central Government 
has after considering the said report , decided to acquire 
the right of user in the lands specified in the schedulo ap 
pended to this notification ; 

NOW , THEREFORE , in exercise of the power conferred 
by sub -section (1 ) of the section 6 of the said Act , the Cen 
tral Government hereby declares that the right of user in 
the said lands specified in tho schedule appended to this 
notification hereby acquired for laying the pipelines ;

अतः अब , पैट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार 
का अर्जन ) अर्धािनयम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा (1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए केन्द्रीय 
सरकार ने उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का अपना 
आशय एतद्द्वारा घोषित किया है । 

शरीक उक्त भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति , उस भूमि के 
नीचे पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए आक्षेप समक्ष प्राधिकारी तेल सथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस आयोग निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाव म्करपुरा रोड, 
परोदा-9 को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर 
सकेगा । 

और ऐसा आक्षेप करने वाला हर यक्ति विनिर्दष्ट यह भी कथन 
करेगा कि क्या वह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई भ्यक्तिशः हो या 
किसी विधि व्यवसायों की मार्फत ।

S . O . 641. — WHEREAS by a notification of the Govt. of 
India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Chemicals (Depart 

ment of Petroleum) S . O . No. 3182 dated 18- 9 - 1972, under 
sub -section ( 1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum Pipelines 
(Acquisition of Right of User in land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 
1962 ), the Central Government declared its intention to 
acquirc thc Right of User in the lands specified in the 
schedule appended to that notification for the purpose of lay 
ing pipelines ; 

AND WHEREAS the Competent Authority has under 
sub -section ( 1 ) of section 6 of the said Act, submitted report 
to the Government; 

AND FURTHER WHEREAS the Central Government 
has after considering the said report, decided to acquire the 
right of user in the lands specified in the schedule appen 
ded to this notification ; 

NOW , THEREFORE , in exercisc of the power conferred 
by sub- section ( 1 ) of the section 6 of the said Act, the 
Central Government hereby declares that the right of user 
in the said lands specified in the schedule appended to this 
notification hereby acquired for laying the pipelines; 

AND FURTHER in exercise of the power conferred by 
sub-section ( 4 ) of that section, the Central Government 
directs that the right of user in the said landa shall instead 
of vesting in the Central Government vest on this date of 
the publication of this declaration in the Oil & Natural 
Gas Commission free from all encumbrances .

का . आ . 842. - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
लोकहित में यह आवश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में ही . एस . 127 
( के आई डी ) से डी . एस , 49 तक पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए 
पाइपलाइन तेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैस आयोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी 
चाहिए । 

और यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को विछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्धारा अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का 

अधिकार अर्जित करना आवश्यक है । 
___ अतः अय , पैट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार 
का अर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा (1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयांग करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
सरकार ने उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जत करने का अपना 
आशय एतद्द्वारा घोषित किया है । 

वश कि उक्त भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई घ्यक्ति , उस भूमि के 
नीचे पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए आक्षेप समक्ष प्राधिकारी संल तथा 


अष , अत : उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा (1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित 
करती है कि इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन विछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिये एतद्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है और , आगे उस 
धारा की उपधारा (4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए 
केन्द्रीय सरकार निदेश दत्ती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का 
अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में विहित होने के बजाय भारतीय तेल

करती है कि इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के

धारा की उपधारा (4) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए

की इस सारीख को निहित होगा ।

के ऊपर की भोर भारतीय मानक की पदसंख्या दी हुई है ।

APPENDIX OF EVIDENCE

Documents ciblblted For Einployers 


Sawyers were in old Category V and that he does not know 
what new Category was given to the Muccadams in the 
Timber Yard . No doubt some evidence also has been let in 
through this witness to show that the work of Timber cut 
ting has come down and that there is not much work for 
tho Sawyers and there has been also somo cross . examination 
on this aspect but this aspect of the matter is not relevant 
for the purpose of the present enquiry , because the whole 
question is whether giving of now Category III to the 
claimants is justified or not. 

9. Now from the evidence referred to it is clear that the 
clajmants were previously working under a contract and 
that later on the Company absorbed them , that they were 
designated as Band - Saw Cutters, that they were placed in 
old Category V , that their main work is only of cutting 
timber into required sizes and that incidentally they are 
also sharpening the teeth of the saw blades and also solder 
ing tho saw blades whenever necessity arose , and that the 
Wago Board did not mako any specific recommendations so 
far as these Band -Saw Cutters are concerned and that 
at the time of implementation of the recommendations of 
tho Wage Board the Management designated thọ climants as 
Sawyers and placed them in new category III and that the 
management has also placed in category III other workers who 
were in old categories IV and V . It is now seen from pago 
169 of Volume I of the recommendations of tho Wage Board 
that as Regards Wage Structure the Wage Board had 
recommended that the existing ten categories should be com 
pressed into six categories as shown in Appendix V and this 
Appendix V is contained in page 41 to 30 of Volume II of 
tho recommendations of the Wage Board . A peruşal of Ap 
pendix V shows that the Wago Board had recommended cale 
gory II for Semi-skilled lower and Category III for Semi 
skilled higher. In as much as the Wage Board has not made 
any specific recommendations 80 far as Band - Saw Cutters 
are concerned , considering the nature of the work done by the 
claimants, the Management had fixed them in new Category III . 
Even W . W . 1 admits that their work has no comparision with 
tho work of Blackamiths and Carpenters So if tho Blacksmiths 
and Carpenters are given some higber category considering the 
Daturo of the work done by them , tho claimants cannot claim 
equal category mercly on the ground that sharpening teeth 
of the blades and soldering the saw blades is the blacksmith s 
work that they are doing and that the cutting of timber 
also is the work like the carpenter s work and so when the 
blacksmiths and carpenters were given new category IV 
they also should be given Dew Catogory IV . 

10 . From the evidence it is seen that the nature of work 
dono by the claimants is only semi- skilled work and even 
if it is assumed that for sharpening teeth of the saw blades 
and for soldering the saw blades some skill is required then 
their work would come only under semi-skilled higher in 
instead of semi-skillel lower and now it is seen that the Wago 
Board has recommended Category III for the workers doing 
tho work of Semi- skillel higher . No doubt it is also now 
seen from the evidence that when similar requests were made 
by tho Sawyers of Kothagudem and Yellandu the Government 
of India had turned down their request observing that tho 
Government of India did not consider the dispute fit for re 
forenco and no doubt in view of this fact it is contended for 
the respondent that the claim of the potitioners also is liable 
to be rejected, but simply because the Government of India 
did not think it fit to refer such a disputo to this tribunal it 
does not mean that the present claimants are debarred from 
putting forward their claim . Now the matter has come up 
before this Tribunal, and it is for this Tribunal to give a 
finding as to whether the Management is justified in placing 
tho claimants in new Category III, after considering the ovi 
donco placed before it. Now from the evidence let in this 
caso it is clear that the nature of the work done by the 
claimants is at best only that of semi- skllled higher , but 
it cannot be said that their work is that of a skilled junior. 
It is now seen that the Wago Board had recommended cate 
gory IV to skilled junior. On a consideration of the whole 
evidence in this case and also the recommendations of the 
Wage Board I am satisfied that considering the nature of 
the work done by the claimants the Management had rightly 
fitted the claimants in Category III new . 

11. For all the aforesaid reasons I hold on the dispute re 
ferred to for adjudication that the Management of Bellampalli 
and Ramakrishnapur Divisions of Mossrs. Singareni Col 
Herion Company Limited , is justified in placing Sarvasbri 
Nagula Chandraiah , Kannaboyina Posham , Kusina Lingaiah , 
Dandi Mondi,Gujja Bucham , Band Saw Cutters in Category

the employers in relation to the Indian Airlines and its 
workmen represented by Indian Flight Engineers Association , 
Bombay ;

a written agreement in pursuance of the provisions of sub 

section ( 1 ) of section 10A of the Industrial Dispute Act , 
___ 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , agrecd to refer the said dispute to arbitra

arbitration agreement has been forwarded to the Central 
Government; 


Now , therefore , in pursuance of sub -section ( 3 ) of section 
10A of the said Act, the Central Government hereby pub 
lishes the said arbitration agreement which was received by 
it on the 3rd February , 1973 .

The proceedings in the present reference boforo mc have 
been some what protracted . Earlier, 30 workers of the 
South Jhagrakhand Collieries (hereinafter called the Jhagril 
kband Collieries ) presented an application , No, 200 of 1968 , 
under Sec. 33 - C ( 2 ) of thc Industrial Disputes Act 1947 
(hereinafter called the Act ) . In the said application the ap 
plicants claimed the difference in the Variable Dearneys Al 
lowance as determined in the Central Wage Board Award 
which they contended was due to them . The operative por 
tion of the order of this Court dated 22 - 3 - 1969 runs thus : 
" The result is that all the applicants are entitled to the 

sum of Rs. 75 .60 P . as claimed till the date of an 
plication . They shall further be entitled to Rs. 5 / 
each in all Rs. 150 / - as costs of the proceedings 
from the opposite party . "

AWARD 


The aforesaid order was challenged by the management 
of the Thagrakhand Collieries by way of it writ petition be 
forc the High Court of Madhya Pradesh . It was dismissed 
by the High Court by its order dated 21- 9 - 1971 . 

It may be mentioned that the application under Sec. 33 - C ( 2 ) 
in Casc No. CGIT / LC ( C ) (472 ) 1970 which was decided by 
this Court on 7 - 1 - 1970 had been opposed by the manage 
ment on the ground , inter alia , that a settlement had been 
arrived at in conciliation procecdings between the manage 
ment and another Union , other than the M . P . Collicry 
Workers Federation which had sponsored the dispute in the 
aforesaid application , and the affect of the settlement was 
that the matter had come to an end on account of the afore 
şaid agreement in concilation proceedings. It was contended 
on behalf of the workmen that Sri B . D . Sharma before whom 
the alleged settlement was said to have been arrived at was 
not a duly appointed Conciliation Officer on the date when 
the settlement was said to have been arrived at between the 
parties . This Court hold that Sri B . D . Sharma was not a 
(July appointed Conciliation Officer on the material date . 
The question was raised before thc High Court and the 
vicw of this Court was upheld by the High Court and it held 
that the aforesaid conciliation proceedlings were of no affect 
and the settlement arrived at in such proceedings was not 
binding on all the workers who were not parties to those 
proceedings. It further cxpressed the view that the afore 
said settlement was not binding " upon the respondent work 
crs because they had alrcady made an application before the 
Labour Court and while this lis wus pending, it was not open 
to the management to settle with other workers and to make 
the settlement bincling upon the workers who had gone before 
the Labour Court and thus to frustrate their proceedings . 
In the result the High Court dismissed the writ pctition , It 
appears that the aforesaid order of the Iligh Court has been 
subjected to all appeal which is pending before the Supreme 
Court of India . The Supreme Court has also stayed further 
proceedings in the suid application under Sec . 33- C ( 2 ) of tho 
Act. As against the aforesaid order of the High Court dated 
21- 9 - 1971 it was stated before mc that an application for 
leave to appeal from the aforesaid Order before the Supremo 
Court of India is pending before the High Court. 

The management has challenged , the competency of the 
reference before mc, particularly on the ground that the 
Central Wage Board recommendations for Coal Mining In 
dustries on which the claim of the workmen is based related 
to about 800 collicrics in different States of India and the 
question involved in the reference being one of National in 
portance and an inter state matter should not have been 
referred to this Tribunal and instead the appropriate forum 
for the consideration of the disputo raised in the referenco 
before me should have been a National Tribunal, There may 
be some force in this contention but in vicw of the awards 
based on mutual settlements that have been given by me in 
the three other references in which the same question was

Thus the aforesaid documents were sought to be proved 
by the management by an allidavit of Shri G . R . Bhandari. 
It was open to the workmen either to challenge the suffi 
ciency of the proof of the aforesaid documents or to have 
demanded the production of the originals of any of the 
copics of the documents which had been filed by the 
management. Rulc 15 of the Rules of the Act says that 
this Tribunal " may accept, admit or call for evidence at 
any stage of the proceedings before it and in such manner 
as it may think fit ." In the present casc documents had 
been filed by the management in full knowledge of the 
workmen s representative. He did not specifically challengo 
the proof of the documents by the affidavit of the manage 
ment, nor did he raise any objection to the aforesaid 
documents being accepted by the Tribunal by its order 
dated 19 - 12 - 1972 . It was open to the representative of the 
workmen to have raised an objection that proof by an 
affidavit was not sufficient and the management should pro 
ducc further evidence to prove the documents which had 
heen produced by it. No such thing was done and all 
that was contended on behalf of the workmen was that the 
management " should not be allowed to introduce irrelevant 
material" before the Tribunal. It was for the Tribunal to 
sce whether the evidence was material or not. So far as 
the admissibility of the documenty was concerned it WAS 
not challenged on behalf of the workmen The Supremo 
Court in Sindhu Resettlement Corporation , Ltd . Vs . Indus 
trial Tribunal, Gujarat, and others ( 1968 - I-LIJ p . 834 ) took 
the view that in proceedings before the Industrial Tribunal 
strict proof of documents in accordance with the provisions 
of the Indian Evidence Act is not required . There 
is , therefore , no force in the contention of the workmen s 
representative that the aforesaid documents had not been 
proved and therefore they should not be accepted in evi 
dence

The learned representative of the workmen placed reliance 
on Burn & Company Ltd . Vs. Their workmen ( A . I. R . 1957 
(SC ) 38) and Walford Transport Ltd . Vs. First Industrial 
Tribunal of West Bengal and others ( 1963 AIR (Calcutta ) 
275 ) in support of the contention that principle of regjudi 
cola applies to industrial disputes and the decision of this 
Court and the M . P . High Court in the earlier applications 
under Sec . 33 - C (2 ) of the Act should be accepted as fina), 
Apart from the question whether the aforesaid orders of 
this Court and the M . P . High Court in proceedings under 
Sec . 33- C (2 ) of the Act have any binding effect in pro 
ceedings under Sec. 10 of the Act , it is now well settled 
that technical considerations of reajudicata cannot be regid 
ly followed in industrial disputes . In ( 1964- I-LLJ p . 380 ) 
Workmen of Balmer Lawrie & Co . vs . Balmer Lawrie 
& Co . Ltd . and another, the Supreme Court obscrvod :- - - 


Thc Dianagement havc filed a copy of the conciliation 
settlement dated 22 - 10 - 1969 . copies of the aforcgaid settle

of the settlement dated 21- 10 - 1972 between the manage

Koyla Mazdur Sabha in which it was agreed that the demand 
relating to the Variablo Dearness Allowance shall be scttled by 
bipartito talks . In an affidavit which was filed along with 
the aforesaid documents it was stated that the documents 
which had been produced were copies of the relevant docu 
ments . The learned representative of the workmen objected 
to tho admissibility of the aforesaid documents. The said 
documents gupported by an affidavit were filed before ine 
on 19 - 12 - 1972 . The order sheet sayA — 


“ While dealing with the question about the revision of 

Wage Scales , it is necessary to remember that 
the technical considerations of resjudicata should 
not be allowed to hamper the discretion of indus 
trial adjudication ." 


Again , the Supreme Court in the case of Shahdara Saharan 
pur Light Railway Vs. Shahdara Saharanpur Railway 
Workers Union ( 1969-I- LLJ p . 734 ) observed : -- 
" the trend in recent decisions is that application of 

technical rules such as resjudicata , acquiescnco , 
estoppel etc . are not appropriate to industrial 

adjudication ." 
Therefore , where the circunstances so warrant, this Tribunal 
would not be ſettered by the tochnical rules of resjudicata

" Ordinarily there should be no difficulty about the

Furthermore , an industrial tribunal acting under Sec . 10 ( 1) 
of tho Act is not bound by any contract of service in de 
ciding a reference . The Supreme Court in Western India

( 1949 -LLJ p . 245) observed :

" Adjudication does not, in our opinion , mean adjudi 

cation According to the strict law of master and 
servant. The award of the tribunal may contain 
provision for settlement of a dispute which no 
court could order if it was bound by ordinary 
law , but the tribunal is not fettcred in any way 
by these limitations ." 


There are about 49 ccllieries in the State of Madhya

The same view was expressed by the Supreme Court in 
Rohtas Industries Ltd . Vs. Brijnandan Pandey & others 
( 1956 - II-LLJ 444). It was observed : 
" An industrial tribunal is not 30 fottered and may 

create new obligations or modify contracts in the 
interests of industrial pcacc . . .

It has been contended on behalf of the management that 
the conditions in the aforesaid collieries and the Jhagrakhand 
Collieries being similar there is no reason why thero should 
he variations in the above rates with regard only in the 
Jhagrakhand Collieries . It was argued that under the terms 
of settlement of 1969 which was subsequently agreed upon 
individually by the workers of the Jhagrakhand Collieries 
the workers were given less favourable terms as compared 
to the terms which were latcr mutually agreed upon between 
the parties in the aforesaid three references , but now the 
management was willing to pay the Variable Dearness 
Allowance in accordance with the aforesaid rates which 
were agreed upon between the parties in the aforesaid thrce 
references . It was , however , contended that under the 
terms of 1969 settlement the workmen were paid at the 
rato of Rs. 1. 29 per day from 1 - 4 - 1968 to 31- 7 -1968 instead 
of Rs. 1 . 11 per day as agreed upon in the settlements of 
the aforesaid three references . It was argued on behalf of 
the workmen that the Variable Dcarness Allowance had 
been fixed earlier by the management of the jhagrakhand 
Collieries and it had become a condition of servicc and any 
change was permissible only on the basis of altered condi 
tions and there was no justification for the management 
to make any change in tho conditions of service of the 
workmen . It was also stressed that since the management 
was now admittedly paying Rs. 2 . 13 per day there was no 
TCASOn why payments of the Variable Dearness Allowance 
should be paid at a lesser rate for the period of the claim . 
As mentioned above, it has come in evidence that the 
management of the Jhagrakhand Collieries had duly issued 
a notice under Sec . 9A of the Act and there was a fresh 
contract between the workmen and the management after 
the settlement of 1969 as a result of the individual workers 
having agreed to receive payment at the rates mutually 
agreed upon by the parties . There is nothing to provent 
this Tribunal from giving effect to a settlement between 


It is not possible for me to overlook the fact that disputes 
involving the same question which is now before me wore 
mutually settled between the parties in the aforesaid threo 
references and I have given my awards in terms of the 
settlements arrived at between the parties . Any variation 
from the aforesaid terms of settlements would create glar 
ing anomaly in tho rates of Variable Dearness Allowance 
payable to the workmen and is bound to raiso industrial 
unrest. This Tribunal has to see that harmony and peace 
is maintained . Furthermore , the principle of uniformity 
cannot be overlooked . Under these circumstances , I am not 
prepared to make any distinction in tho rates of the Vari 
able Dearness Allowance paid to the workmen of tho 
Jhagrakhand Collieries and the workmen of the other 
neighbouring collierica in which the namo question which is 
now in issue beforo mc was mutually settled between the 
parties as mentioned above . I, therefore , consider it proper 
that the terms with regards to rates of the Variable Dear 
ness Allowance in the aforesaid three references should 
also be made applicable in the present reference . The 
aforesaid terms have, however , to be varied to the extent 
that they are brought in conformity with the rates of Vari 
able Dcarness Allowance payable to the workmen as 
directed in my awards in the aforesaid thrco references . 


My answer to the reference, therefore , is that Variable 
Dearness Allowance shall be paid to the workmen on rolls 
of the Colliery and at the rates as mentioned below :- -

by his letter dated 10th December, 1970 (Ex . M - 1) that the 
enquiry into the charge was to be held at the Central Office 
at Madras on 17 - 12 - 1970 and that he should appear for the 
enquiry and let in evidence , if he had any in his defence . 
The President of the All India Overseas Bank Employees 
Union who was defending the employee wrote a letter 
dated 20 - 8 - 1971 giving a list of witnesses to be examined 
on behalf of the employce (Ex. M - 2 ). The third witneys 
inentioned therein was one Sri R . Sankaralingum , working

Officer requested Sri R . Sankaralingam to attend the en 
quiry (Vide Ex, M - 3 ). Sri Sankaralingam attended the on 
quiry and gave evidence on behalf of Sri S . Venugopal. It 
is not denied that Sri Sankaralingam came to Madras from 
Karur and deposod at the domestic enquiry on 20 - 10 - 1971 
and 21- 10 - 1971 . Tho claim now made by tho linion is that 
Sri Sankaralingam should bo paid his travelling and daily 
allowances for the days he came to Madras to give ovi 
dence and to treat the period of his absence en dụty . It 
is averred in the claim statement of the union that since it 
was the Enquiry Officer who called Sri Sankaralingam for 
examination at the enquiry , the bank should bear all the 
expenses incurred by him for appearing at the enquiry and 
should also terat his period of absence as on duty. It is 
stated that all the others who gave cvidence were treated 
as on duty and that this amounts to discrimination and un 
fair labour practice . It is further alleged that whenever 
any member of the staff of the bank was examined for an 
enquiry held by the bank he would be paid halting allowance 
and travelling allowance apart from trcating his period of 
absence as on duty , and that this has become a condition 
of service of an employee which cannot be altered without 
following the relevant provisions of the Industrial Disputes 
Act. 

(3 ) The contention of the union that Sri Sankaralingam 
W88 called by the Enquiry Officer to give evidence and that 
he would not have attended the enquiry , but for such re 
quest is not tonable. It is clear from Exs. M - 2 and M - 3 
that it was only because the delinquent employee wanted 
to examino Sri Sankaralingam among others on his behalf 
that the Enquiry Officer asked him to appear for the on 
quiry . In other words, he was examined at the instance 
of the union on behalf of the delinqucnt employee at the 
enquiry . The Enquiry Officer had merely forwarded the 
request of the union . In a domestic enquiry , it is for the 
charge - sheeted worker to take steps to produce witnesses 
before the Enquiry Offlcer . Here , the " Enquiry Officer 
appears to have assisted the delioquent employee by writing 
a letter like Ex . M - 3 . It cannot therefore be said that Sri 
Sankaralingam came to give evidence at the domestic on 
quiry as he was asked to do so by the bank . 

(4 ) It is further contended in the claim statement that 
out of the six witnesses examined at the enquiry , all except 
Sri R . Sankaralingam were treated as on duty and that 
therefore this is discrimination. But the other witnesses 
were working in Madras , the place of enquiry and therefore 
they appear to have been granted permission . The pre 
vailing practice in the bank appears to be that only witnesses 
who are examined for the bank at a domestic enquiry are 
entitled to duty leave and halting and travelling allowances . 
It is urged for the union that the denial of such allowances 
and leave to a witness examined for a delinquent employee 
at the domestic enquiry amounts to discrimination and un 
fair labour practice. This contention cannot be accepted , 
In paragraph 17 - 12 of thc settlement dated 14 - 12 - 1966 
(Ex . M -6 ), which is still in force, the procedure for any 
disciplinary action against an employee has been laid down . 
Para, 17 . 12( d ) which is relevant for our purpose is ag 
follows:

Disputcs Act, 1947, ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby published the following award of the Central Govern 
ment Industrial Tribunal, No. 2 , Bombay on preliminary 
objection , in the industrial dispute between the employers in

V . SANKARALINGAM , Under Secy . 


(8 ) It is no doubt true that it is open to an industrial 
tribunal to create now rights and obligations between the 
parties as in settling the disputes between tho employer 
and the workmen , the function of the Tribunal is not con 
fined to administration of justice in accordance with law , 
But this jurisdiction is not arbitrary . In Punjab National 
Bank , Ltd ., Vs. Industrial Tribunal, Delhi, and others ( 1) . 
His Lordship Justice S . K . Das observed as follows at 
page 458:- - 
" It may be conceded that the jurisdiction of an in 

dustrial tribunal is not invoked for the enforcement 
of mere contractual rights and liabilities of the 
partics to tho dispute referred to the tribunal for 
adjudication ; its jurisdiction in the matter of ad 
judication of an industrial dispute is wider And 
more flexible . All the same, it is not an arbitrory 
jurisdiction ; it may be readily conceded that an 
employee is as much entitled to a fair deal as 
an employer and he must be protected from victi 
mization and unfair labour practice , but " social 
justico" does not mean that reason and fairness 
must always yield to the convenience of a party 
convenience of the omployce at the cost of the 
employer as in this case in an adjudication pro 
ceeding. Such one- sided or partial view is really 
next- of- kin to caprice or humour. "

AWARD ON PRELIMINARY OBJECTION 


By Order No. L - 36011 / 4 /71- P & D dated 14 - 9 - 1971 the 
Central Government in the Ministry of Labour and Rehabili 
fation (Department of Labour and Employment) in exercise of 
the powers conferred by clause ( d ) of sub -section ( 1 ) of 
Section 10 of the 1. D . Act , 1947 ( 14 of 1947) referred to 
this Tribunal for adjudication an industrial dispute existing 
between the employers in relation the Stevedores at Mormu 
yao Harbour , Mormugao and their workmen in respect of the 
matters specified in the Schedule as mentioned below : 


" SCHEDULE 


Whether the demand of the gangmen marked as I, in 

the records of the Mormugao Dock Labour Board 
for being placed in the scale of Rs. 135- 4 - 163 - 5 - 178 
with effect from the 1st January , 1969 in the light 
of the recommendations of the Central Wage Board 
for Port and Dock Workers, is justificd ? If not, 
to what relief if any, are the workmen entitled ? " 


barred by reason of the provisions of the Act and 

the Scheme as alleged , 
(ii) The present dispute is a dispute between the Stevedores 

on one hand and their employees on the other . The 
Stevedores carry on the Stevedoring industry and 
therefore the dispute between the Stevedores und 
their workmen is an industrial dispute. When such 
dispute is referred to an Industrial Tribunal for 
adjudication , it has jurisdiction to consider and adju 
dicate upon the demands made by the workmen 
against their employers . The Provisions of the Act 
and Scheme do not bar the authority of an Indus 
trial Tribunal to adjudicate upon an industrial dispute 

between the Dock Workers and their employers . 
( iii) The Board constituted under the Scheme is not the 

employer of the Dock Workers. The said Board is 
a Statutory Board charged with the duty of adminis 
tering the scheme, the object of which is to ensure 
the greater regularity of the employment for Dock 
Workers and to ensure that adequate number of 
Dockworkers are available for efficient perforinance of 
the Dock work . Though the Board is competent to 
determine and prescribe the wages and allowances 
and other conditions of service of the Dock Workers , 
it has no powers and authority to adjudicate upon 
the dispute between Dock Workers and their cmp 
loyers relating to wages and allowances and ointr 
conditions of service . It has only the power to 
endeavour to settle disputes about which a request 
for adjudication has been made 10 the Central 
Government by the parties concerned and to report 
to the Govt . the results of such cndeavours. This 
clearly shows that there can be an adjudication of 
a dispute between the Dock Employers and their 
Dock Workers. The Board has no power to adjudi 
cate upon them . The power of adjudication lics 

only with an Industrial Tribunal. 
( iv ) The wages and allowances determined by the Board 

can be a cause of a dispute and either the Employers 
or the Dock Workers can raisc an industrial dispute , 
with regard to the wages and allowances determined 
by the Board . The power and the authority of an 
Indstrial Tribunal to adjudicate upon an industrial 
dispute between the Dock workers and theſr emp 
loyers is not taken away by the provisions of Sec 
tion 8 of the Dock Workers (Regulation of Employ 
ment) Act, 1946 Clauses 42 and 43 of the Scheme 
clearly show that the rates of wages and allowances 
etc . fixed by the Board form an implied condition 
of contract between the registered Dock Workers 
and the Registered Employers. An industrial adju 
dication has the power to vary the said contract 
whether implied or express when an industrial dispute 
in respect of any term of the same is referred to 
It for adjudication , 


2 .. Messrs . V . S . Dempo and Company and 11 other Steve 
dores thereinafter referred to as the Employers ) have filed 
common written statement at Ex. 4 / E raising preliminary 
objections to the maintainability of the reference 


3 . According to them : 


(i) This reference is bad in law and not malntainable us 

this Tribunal s jurisdiction to adjudicatc on a dispute 
regarding wages of any dock workers under the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947, is impliedly barred 
by reason of the provisions of thc Dock Workers 
(Regulation of Employment ) Act , 1948 (hereinafter 
referred to as "thc Act ) and the Mormugao Dock 
Workers (Regulation of Employment) Schemc 1965 

(hereinafter referred to as the " the Scheme ) 
( ii ) Under the Act the Mormugao Dock Labour Board 

(hereinafter referred to as the Board ) is the only 
competent authority to determine the wages and 

conditions of service of Dock Workers . 
(iii) The Board is an autonomous body competent to deter 

mine and prescribe the wages , allowances and other 
conditions of strvice of the dock workers . The 
registered cmployers do not have the freedom of 
prescribing the rates of wages and other terms and 
conditions of service. The power to determinc wages 
and other service conditions is exclusively within the 
ambit of the powers of the Board. It is not open 
to this Tribunal nor has this Tribunal, any juris 
diction to determine the wages and service condi 

tions of any dock workers . 
(iv ) Jurisdiction of the Tribunal to create a new contract 

between disputed parties can only be exercised if 
the employer is himself capable of contracting terms 
and conditions of employment. In the present case 
the employers have no jurisdiction nor power by 
reason of the said Act and Scheme to determine and 
fix wages and other scrvice conditions, and there 
fore , there can be no adjudication of the present 
dispute as the employers concerned are not compe 
tent to create any contract.

15. There can be no doubt from the abovo mentioned

body competent to determine and prescribe the wages, allow 
ance and other conditions of service of the Dock Workers, 
This ruling also lays down that the Board cannot be con 
sidered as the Employer of the Dock Workers and there is 
no relationship of master and servant between the two. It 
also lays down that the Board s function under the Act and 
the Scheme is not to carry on any industry do as to attract 
the provision of the I. D . Act, 1947.

Rs. 110 - 2 . 50 - 120 - 5 - 147 with effect from 1 - 1 -1969, which 
scale is recommended by the Wage Board for the gang 
workers till such time the dispute regarding their scale of 
pay is finally resolved by the Central Government, there 
cannot be any doubt that the Board is recognising the Gang 
workers named as L . It also appears from the resolution 
that the Board had decided to give a particular scale of pay 
to these persons. till their scale of pay is finally resolved by 
the Central Government. In view of this it is clear that 
there is no substance in the contention raised by Shri Onka 
rappa saying that any award or order granting increased 


and their Dock Workers , that the Board has no 
power to adjudicate upon them , that the power of 
adjudication Jics only with an Industrial Tribunal 
to whom such a dispute is referred for adjudication 
by the Central Government and that the wages 
and allowance determined by the Board can be a 

cause of a dispute and , 
( vi ) cither the Employers or the Dock Workers can 

raise an industrial dispute with regard to the wages 
and allowances determined by the Board . 


22 . Clause 8 ( f ) of the Scheme lays down that the Board 
in meeting shall be responsible for dealing with all matters 
of policy and in particular may determine the wages , allow 
ances and other conditions of service and refix the guaran 
teed minimum wages in a month after annual review . 


of separate category of Gangleaders and amendment to the 
Schedulc I of thic Scheme. 


23 . It is clear from clause 8 ( f ) that the Board may deter 
mine the wages , allowances and other conditions of service 
of Dock Workers. This clause does not lay down that the 
Board shall determine the wages . 


24 . Shri Rele contends that clause 8 ( f ) has to be read 
alongwith clause 42 ( a ) of the Scheme and that on reading 
the clauses together it will be clcar that the word may in 
clause 8 will have to be construed as "shall , 


19 . Learned Advocate Shri Rele for the Stevedores con 
tends that this Tribunal has 110 power to determine the 
wages , allowance and conditions of service of Dock Workers 
because the power to determine wages and conditions of 
service of Dock Workers is exclusively within the ambit of 
the power of the Board under the provisions of the Dock 
Workers (Regulation of Employment ) Act, 1948 and the 
Mormugao Dock Workers (Regulation of Employment) 
Schemc 1965 , framed thereunder , and that on account of 
this , this tribunal has no jurisdiction to adjudicate upon the 
dispute between the stevedores and the Dock Workers l e 
garding their wages etc . In support of this contention 
Shri Rele relies on clauses 8 (f ) and 42 ( a ) of the Scheme 
and two rulings viz . i ) Ruling of Patna High Court in the 
case between Sarat Chattarjee and Co. (Private ) Itd . and 
others and the Central Govt. Industrial Tribunal , Dhanbad 
and others reported in 1963, ILLJ, Page 76 and ( 2 ) Ruling 
of Supreme Court in the case between Vegoils Private Ltd . 
and the workmen reported in 1971 , II,LLI, page 567, rele 
vant portion at page 579 , para . 44 . 


25 , Shri Sowani on tho other hand contends that if two 
words appear in the same sentence or clausc , the intention 
of the legislature is clear. According to him , inasmuch as 
two words shall and may appear in clause 8 of the Scheme, 
the word may will have to be construed as permissive and 
not as shall i.e . binding. If the legislature wanted that the 
Board shall determine the wages , it would have used the 
word shall and not may . 


26 . There is much force in what Shri Sowani says . As 
two words shall and may appear in the same clause 8 of 
the scheme, the intention of the legislature is that the Board 


from this that exclusive jurisdiction for determining wages 
etc. has not been given to the Board . In any case the 
power to Adjudicate the dispute regarding wages etc . between 
the Stevedores and the Dock Workers cannot be said to have 
been taken away by this clause . . 


20 . It is contended on behalf of the Union that the 
power and authority of an Industrial Tribunal to adjudicate 
upon an industrial dispute between the Dock Workers and 
their Employers is not taken away by the provisions of 
Section 8 of the Mormugao Dock Workers (Regulation of 
Employment) Scheme 1965, that clauses 42 and 43 of the 
Scheme clearly show that the rates of wages allowances ctc . 
fixed by the Board form an implied condition of contract 
between the registered Dock Workers and the Registered 
Employers , and that an Industrial Tribunal has the power 
to vary the said contract whether implied or express when 
an industrial dispute in respect of any term of the same is 
referred to it for adjudication. 


27 . Clause 8 ( 0 ) of the Scheme lays down that the Board 
in particular may endeavour to settle disputes about which - a 
request for adjudication has been made to the Central Gov 
ernment by the parties concerned and report to the 
Government the results of such endeavours. 


28 . In the caso reported in 1963, ILLJ, page 76 , between 
Sarat Chatterjcc and Co . (Pvt. ) Ltd . and others and the 
Central Government Industrial Tribunal, Dhabad and others 
before the Patna High court and relied upo by Shri Relç 
it is held as follows : -- 


21. According to the Union : 
( i) the Mormugao Dock Labour Board constituted under 
· the Mormugao Dock Workers (Regulation of 

Employment ) Scheme 1965 is not the employer of 

the Dock Workers . 
(ii ) the said Labour Board is a statutory Board charged 
. with the duty of administering the scheme, the 

object of which is to ensure the greater regularity 
. . of employment for Dock Workers and to ensure 

that adequate number of Dock Workers are avail . 
able for the efficient performance of the Dock 
Work , 


" Şub -clauec ( 0 ) of Çl. 8 of the Scheme no doubt 

postulates that in spite of the scheme there could 
be disputes for which request for adjudication 
could be made to the Central Government by the 
parties concerned and the Government in its turn 
will have to refer the industrial dispute for adjudi 
cation by an appropriate Tribunal. But this sub 
clause would not cover the disputes which are within 
the exclusivo jurisdiction of the Board , under 
Cls. 8 ( f ) and 41 of the Scheme." (Calcutta 
Scheme) . " 


Sec . 3(ii)]

whether implied or express when an industrial dispute in 
respect of any terms of it is referred for adjudication . Hence 
the contention raised by Shri Rele fails . 


29. In vicw of the above observations of the Patna High 
Court there can be no doubt that in spite of the Scheme 
1.e . Mormugao Dock Workers (Regulation of Employment) 
Scheme 1965 there could be industrial dispute for which a 
request for adjudication could be made to the Central 
Government by the parties concerned and the Government 
in its turn will have to refer the industrial dispute for adjudi 
cation by an appropriate Tribunal . 


36 . Registered Dock Workers are supplied to the Steve 
dores by the Board . It is clearly observed by the Lordships 
of Supreme Court in para 24 on page 55 in the case bet 
ween Visakhapatnam Dock Labour Board Vs. Stevedores 
Association reported in 1970 , ILLJ, as follows: 


30 . In the present casc I have already observed that clause 
8 ( f ) does not give exclusive jurisdiction to the Board for 
determining the wages etc . What clause 8 ( f ) shows is that 
the Board in particular may determine the wages etc . As 
tho expression used in the clause is may I am of the view 
that the jurisdiction of the Tribunal to adjudicate upon the 
industrial dispute regarding wages etc . of the Dock Workers 
is not taken away by this clause. 


31. In thc Patna High Court ruling reported in 1963, 
I,LLJ, Page 76 it is observed that the workers in the Reserve 
Pool are considered the employees of the Board but in view 
of the Supreme Court ruling referred to in pusa . 14 above , 
the Board is not the employer of the dock workers and 
it is not carrying on any industry . 


32 . Shri Rele relies on para , 44 at page 579 of the Supreme 
Court ruling reported in 1971, II,LLJ, page 567. That 
paragraph is as follows: 


" 44 . The legality of the direction given by the Indus 

trial Tribunal abolishing contract labour in respect 
of loading and unloadig from May 1 , 1971 can 
also be considered from another point of view . The 
Central Act, as mentioned earlier had come into 
force on February 10 , 1971. Under S . 10 of the 
said Act the jurisdiction to decide matters connected 
with prohibition of contract labour is now vested in 
the appropriate Government. Therefore , with effect 
from February 10 , 1971, it is only the appropriate 
Government that can prohibit contract labour by 
following the procedure and in accordance with 
the provisions of the Central Act. The Industrial 
Tribunal, in the circumstances will have no juris 
diction , though its award is dated November 20 , 
1970 , to give a direction in that respect which 
becomes eforceable after the date of the coming 
into force of the Central Act. In any cvent, such 
A direction contained in the award cannot bo cn 
dorced from a date when abolition of contact 
labour can only be done by the appropriate Gov 
ernment in accordance with the provisions of the 
central Act. In this view also it must be held that 
the direction of the Industrial Tribunal abolishing 
contract labour with effect from May 1 , 1971, re 
garding loading and unloading cannot be suis 
tained ." 


" 24 . All these circumstances in our opinion, prima facie 

establish that the Board cannot be considered to 
be the employer of the Dock Labour workmen . In 
fact the various provisions referred to in the 
Scheme, clearly show that the registered employer 
to whom the labour force is allotted by the Board 
is the employer whosc work of loading or unload 
ing of ships is done by the dock workers allotted 

to them ." 
37. In view of the above obscrvations of the Supremo 
Court it is clear that the Registered Dock Workers are the 
employces of the Stevedores to whom they are allotted for 
work . Hence the dispute between the Dock Workers and 
the Stevedores who are carrying on business which is an 
industry , will be an industrial dispute within the provisions 
of the I.D . Act, 1947. Hence the dispute between the Steve 
dores and the Dock Workers can be referred to an Industrial 
Tribunal by the Government under the provisions of the 
1.D . Act, 1947 . 
• 38 . In short , it will be clear from the above discussions 
that this Tribunal has jurisdiction to adjudicato the disputo 
between the Stevedores and the Gang Leaders marked as 
" L and that this reference is not bad in law . I therefore 
Pass the following order. 


ORDER 


(i) It is hereby declared that this Tribunal has jurisdic 

tion to adjudicate the present disputo between the 
Stevedores and Dock Workers in question , and 
that the present reference is not bad in law . 


33 . It appears from the facts of the above ruling that 
jurisdiction to decide matters connected with prohibition of 
contract labour Waş vested with the Appropriate Government 
under section 10 of the Central Act. There is however no 
such provision under which the power to adjudicate the 
dispute regarding wages etc . between the Stevedores and 
tho Dock Workers has been vested with the Board , Clause 
8 (f ) of the Mormugao Dock Workers (Regulation of 
Employment) Scheme is only a permissive and not manda 
tory . I am therefore of the view that the ruling in respect 
of abolition of Contract Labour relied upon by Shri Rele 
does not apply to the facts of the present case . 


( ii ) Reference is fixed for taking further steps . 
( iii ) Award on preliminary objection is made accord 
ingly . 

N . K . VANI, Prosiding Officer.

• 34 . Shri Rele contends that the jurisdiction of an Indus 
trial Tribunal to create a new contract between disputed 
parties can only be exercised if the employer is himself 
capable of contracting terms and conditions of employment, 
that in the present case the employers (Stevedores ) have no 
jurisdiction nor power by reason of the Act and the Scheme 
to determine and fix wages and other service conditions of 
tho Dock Workers and that on account of this, this Tribunal 
has no jurisdiction to adjudicate upon the present dispute .

का . आ . 625. यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विदिष्ट विषयों के बारे में राजस्थान सरकार 
के खान और भूविज्ञान विभाग की जामेर कोटरा राक फासफट प्रास्प 
क्टिंग माईन्स के प्रबंध तंत्र से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्म 
कारों के बीच एक औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ।
